tamiḻaṉiṉ piṟantakam
oru vīṭṭiṟku āvaṇam pōṉṟē, oru nāṭṭiṟku varalāṟu urimaic cāṉṟākum. āyiṉ, ōr āvaṇattil etirikaḷāl ētēṉum karavaṭamākac cērkkappaṭalām. atupōṉṟē, orunāṭṭu varalāṟum pakaivarāl avarkkēṟṟavāṟu māṟṟappaṭalām. ātalāl, iv viru vakaiyilum, urimaiyāḷar viḻippāyiruntu tam urimaiyaip pōṟṟik kāttukkoḷḷal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻaka varalāṟu, āriyar teṉṉāṭu vantatiliruntu kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭāka maṟaikkappaṭṭum māṟṟappaṭṭum vantuḷḷatu. itaṉ uṇmaiyāṉa nilaimaiyai aṟitaṟku, intiya varalāṟṟait teṟkiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅkal vēṇṭum. intiya varalāṟṟut tantaiyākak karutappaṭum viṉceṉṟu cimitu (Vincent Smith), tam `intiya muntiya varalāṟu' (Early History of India) pottakattil, pērā. cuntaram piḷḷaiyiṉ kūṟṟaip piṉvarumāṟu eṭuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār:
INDIA PROPER IN THE SOUTH
``The attempt to find the basic element of Hindu civilization by a study of Sanskrit and the history of Sanskrit in Upper India is to begin the problem at its worst and most complicated point. India, South of the Vindhyas the Peninsular India-still continues to be India Proper. Here the bulk of the people continue distinctly to retain their pre-Aryan features, their pre-Aryan languages, their pre-Aryan social institutions. Even here, the process of Aryanization has gone indeed too far to leave it easy for the historian to distinguish the native warp from the foreign woof. But. if there is anywhere any chance of such successful disentanglement, it is in the South: and the farther South we go the larger does the chance Grow.
The scientific historian of  India, then, ought to begin his study with the basin of the Krishna, of the Cauvery, of the Vaigai, rather than with the Gangetic plain, as it has been, now long, too long, the fashion.''			- (Tamilan Antiquary, No. 2 (1908), p.4.)
in neṟimuṟaiyaik kaṭaippiṭittut tamiḻaka varalāṟu varaintavar pi.ṭi. cīnivācaiyaṅkār, vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar eṉṉum iruvarē.
oru nāṭṭu varalāṟu varaitaṟkup payaṉpaṭum mūlavāykaḷ (Sources) piṉvarumāṟu aivakaippaṭum:
1. marapu vaḻakku (Tradition)
2. ayal nāṭṭār kuṟippu
3. tolporuṭ cāṉṟu (Archaeological evidence)
4. nāṭṭilakkiyam
5. nāṭṭumoḻi.
paḻam pāṇṭināṭāṉa paṇṭait tamiḻakam kaṭaluḷ muḻukip pōṉataṉālum, āriyar varukaikku muṟpaṭṭa aṉaittilakkiyamum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭataṉālum tolporuṭ cāṉṟum paṇṭai ilakkiya mum tamiḻaka varalāṟṟiṟkup payaṉpaṭāvāṟu aṭiyōṭu iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl, iṉṟu varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟāka utavuvatu perumpālum moḻiyē.
iṟṟai ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ
tamiḻ tōṉṟiyatu teṉmāvāriyil muḻukippōṉa kumarināṭē. ātalāl, tamiḻaṉ tōṉṟiyatum an nāṭē. kumarikkaṇṭat teṉ kōṭiyil, paṉimalait toṭarpōlum paṉmalaiyaṭukkattuk kumarimalait toṭarum, ataṉiṉṟu pāyntōṭiya paḵṟuḷiyeṉṉum kaṅkai pōlum pēriyāṟum iruntaṉa. ap paḻam pāṇṭināṭṭai yāṇṭa pāṇṭi vēntaruḷ oruvaṉ neṭiyōṉ eṉpāṉ.
``munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ
naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē.'' 	(puṟam. 9: 10-11)
avaṉ kālattilō, piṉpō, oru peruṅ kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻntu, kumarimalaiyum paḵṟuḷiyāṟum ataṉ karaiyiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ talainakarākiya (teṉ) maturaiyum uḷḷiṭṭa paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉperum pakutiyai muḻukkiviṭṭatu. atuvē tamiḻilakkiyaṅ kūṟum mutal kaṭalkōḷ. kaṭalkōḷukkut tappiya allatu piṟpaṭṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ oruvaṉ, vaṭaticaiyiluḷḷa paṉimalaiyaiyum kaṅkaiyāṟṟaiyuṅ kaippaṟṟi, tāṉ iḻanta kumarimalaikkum paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum īṭu ceytukoṇṭāṉ. itaiyē,
``aṭiyiṟ ṟaṉṉaḷa varacark kuṇartti
vaṭivē leṟinta vāṉpakai poṟātu
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmalai yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticaik kaṅkaiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟicai yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi''	(cilap. 11 : 17-22)
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ.
aṭiyiṟ ṟaṉṉaḷa varacark kuṇartti vaṭivēleṟintu vāṉpakai koṇṭavaṉ, neṭiyōṉukku muntiya vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ. neṭiyōṉ munnīrkku viḻāk koṇṭāṭi ataṉoṭu naṭpup pūṇṭavaṉ.
kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉpakuti mūḻkiyapiṉ, vaṭapakutiyil neytal nilattil, katavam (kapāṭam) allatu putavam allatu alaivāy eṉṟu peyar peṟṟa iraṇṭām pāṇṭiyat talainakar tōṉṟiṟṟu. piṉpu ataiyuṅ kaṭal koṇṭatu. aḵtu iraṇṭām kaṭalkōḷ.
ataṟkut tappiya pāṇṭiyaṉ, taṉpōl eñciyirunta taṉ kuṭikaḷaic cōḻanāṭṭilum cēra nāṭṭilum ovvoru pakutiyaik kaippaṟṟik kuṭiyiruttiṉāṉ. itaṉaiyē,
``malitirai yūrntutaṉ maṇkaṭal vauvaliṉ
meliviṉṟi mēṟceṉṟu mēvārnā ṭiṭampaṭap
puliyoṭu vilnīkkip pukaḻpoṟitta kiḷarkeṇṭai
valiyiṉāṉ vaṇakkiya vāṭāccīrt teṉṉavaṉ''	(104 : 1-4)
eṉṟu mullaikkali kūṟum.
``cōḻanāṭ ṭellaiyilē muttūrk kūṟṟamum cēra mānāṭṭuk kuṇṭūrk kūṟṟamum eṉṉum ivaṟṟai, iḻanta nāṭṭiṟkāka āṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ'' eṉṟu aṭiyārkkunallār uraittatum itupaṟṟiyatē.
iṅṅaṉam, iru kaṭalkōḷkaḷāl kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭi yilirunta paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum vaṭakōṭiyilirunta kumariyāṟṟiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa nila nīṭciyai ``eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatavāṟu'' (ēṟattāḻa īrāyiram kal) eṉṟum, an nilapparappup pakutikaḷai, ``ēḻteṅka nāṭum, ēḻmaturai nāṭum, ēḻmuṉpālai nāṭum, ēḻpiṉpālai nāṭum, ēḻ kuṟumpālai nāṭum eṉṉum inta nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭum kumari, kollam mutaliya paṉmalai nāṭum kāṭum natiyum patiyum'' eṉṟum, aṭiyārkku nallār uraittiruppatu, kaṭukaḷavuṅ kaṭṭuc ceytiyāyirukka  muṭiyātu.
aruccuṉaṉ teṟkē tirunīrāṭṭiṟku vanta pāratak kālattilum, ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭa tolkāppiyar kālattilum (ki.mu. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭu) paḵṟuḷi maturai nōkki, vaṭamaturaiyeṉa vaḻaṅkiya vaikai maturai tōṉṟa villai, kumariyāṟu ōṭiṟṟu. piṉṉar atuvum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiṟṟu. ataṉ piṉṉarē, ``toṭiyōḷ pauvam'' eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kuṟikkavum, ``taṭanīrk kumari vaṭaperuṅ kōṭṭiṉkāṟuṅ kaṭalkoṇ ṭoḻitalāl'' eṉṟu aṭiyārkkunallār uraikkavum nērntatu. kumariyāṟṟaik kaṭalkoṇṭatu mūṉṟāṅ kaṭalkōḷ.
maṇimēkalai kālattiṟ pukār kaṭaluṭ pukuntatu nālāṅ kaṭalkōḷ. iṟaiyaṉā rakapporu ḷuraiyiluḷḷa mukkaḻaka varalāṟu, mutaliru kaṭalkōḷkaḷaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. itu nampattakka ceytiyē.
paṉimalai kaṭaluṭ patuṅkiyirunta toṉmutu paṇṭaik kālattil, nāvalantīviṉ teṉpākam uyarntum vaṭapākam tāḻntum iruntaṉa. paṉimalai kiḷarntu kumarimalai nāṭu mūḻkiyapiṉ, vaṭapākam uyarntum teṉpākam tāḻntum, vaṭateṉ ticaikaḷ muṟaiyē uttaram takkaṇam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
o.nō:	kīḻ - kīḻkku - kiḻakku X mēl - mēṟku.
	u (mēl, uyar) + taram = uttaram.
	takku = tāḻvu. takkut toṇṭai = tāḻnta kural toṇṭai.
	takku = takkaṇam (Deccan).
valattai allatu valaticaiyaik kuṟikkum takṣaṇa (L. dexter-dexterity) eṉṉum vaṭacol takṣ mūlattiṉiṉṟu piṟantatātaliṉ, vēṟu collām. māṉiyar uvilliyamcu camaṟkiruta āṅkila akaramutaliyil uttara (uttara) eṉṉum collaip pārkka.
tamiḻ teṟkē tōṉṟiya moḻiyātalāl, teṉmoḻi, teṉkalai, teṉṉāṭu, teṉṉilam, teṉpulam, teṉpulattār, teṉpāli, teṉṉakam, teṉpāṇṭi, teṉmaturai, teṉṉaṉ, teṉṉavaṉ mutaliya colvaḻak keḻuntaṉa.
irāmaṉ taṉ kāṉaka vāḻkkaiyil akattiyaraik kaṇṭapōtu, piṉṉavar muṉṉavaṉai aṉpuṭaṉ varavēṟṟatai, kalviyiṟ periyavarum pāvalaruḷ māvalarumāṉa kampar,
``niṉṟavaṉai vantaneṭi yōṉaṭipa ṇintāṉ
aṉṟavaṉu maṉpoṭuta  ḻīiyaḻuta kaṇṇāṉ
naṉṟuvara veṉṟupala nalluraipa karntāṉ
eṉṟumuḷa teṉṟamiḻi yampiyicai koṇṭāṉ''	(kamparā. akat. 47)
eṉṟu iṉitta taṉittamiḻp pāvāṟ pāṭi, tamiḻiṉ kaṉṉit tūymaiyai yum, teṉkumarināṭṭut tōṟṟattaiyum, eṉṟu maḻiyāt taṉmaiyaiyum, akattiyar ataik kaṟṟut tērntataiyum muttami ḻilakkaṇam iyaṟṟip pukaḻ peṟṟataiyum, oruṅkē teḷḷat terivittār.
The Tamils Indigenous to South India
``Some writers conduct the ancient Dravidians with the self-confidence of a Cook's guide through the North-western or North-eastern mountain passes of India and drop them with a ready-made foreign culture on the banks of the Kaveri or the Vaigai. The slender evidence on which they rely for this elaborate theorizing is the fact that Brahui, a dialect spoken in the northern corner of India, possesses a few words allied to Tamil words. The only legitimate inference from this is that the Tamil language or a language allied to it prevailed upto the North-west province in ancient times. This inference is supported by another  fact, viz., that the modern dialects of Northern India now called Sanskritic or Gaudian, have a fundamental grammatical frame work and a scheme of syntax, the same as that of the Dravidian dialects, so much so that sentences from the one set of dialects can be translated into any one of the other set of dialects by the substitution of word for word without causing any breach of idiom. These facts can only prove that people speaking dialects allied to Tamil once inhabited the whole of India and not that these people must necessarily have come into India from outside the country. No single fact has yet been adduced that compels us to believe that the ancient people of India were not autochthones.''
(The History of the Tamils by P. T. Srinivasa Iyenger, p.2.)
Where This Evolution First Took Place
The five sub-division of the habitable regions occur contiguous to each other and in a small fraction of the earth's surface in India South of the Vindhyas. It is therefore easy to understand how increase of population and alterations in the natural supply of food-stuffs brought about here at different periods the migration of men from region to region and the consequent development of the different stages of human culture, the hunter, the nomad, the pastoral, the coastal and the agricultural, due to the different stimuli provided by the changing milieu; in other words, the geographical control of the growth of human civilization can be worked out and set forth clear as on a map, by a study of man's progress in this restricted portion of the surface of the earth. Outside India, these five natural, regions occur on a vast scale, e.g., the Mullai, the vast steppe land extending from the Carpathians to the foot hills of the Altais, the Kurinji or the great mountain chain from the Pyrenees to the Himalayas and beyond, forming a great girdle round the waist of mother earth the Neydal, the coasts of the Mediterranean sea, and the Indian and the Atlantic oceans, and the Palai, the great desert of Sahara and its continuation in Arabia, Persia and Mongolia''. (Ibid., p.14.)
The myth of the importation of the South Indian languages from outside India.
``Certain investigators assume that everything Indian must have been imported at some remote times from outside India. They hold that the speakers of primitive Tamil and other South Indian languages, to whom they give the name of the Dravidian race, must have settled in India from Central Asia. These theorists drag large bodies of ancient along the map of Asia and Europe as easily as chessmen are shifted on the chess-board, and make them wander aimlessly from country to country, merely to support their baseless theories. It happens that certain words of the Brahui dialect of Baluchistan resemble certain Tamil words. To explain this solitary fact, which is susceptible of various interpretations, they will have it that an ancient Dravidian race, evolved somewhere outside India in the Asiatic highlands, undertook a pilgrimage to India through the North-western Gate, and reached the extreme South through strange and devious ways. Foote constructs an itinerary for the wandering of these peoples, which is a specimen of perverse ingenuity without any historicl evidence to support it. Surely the Neolithians of India were not dumb animals, but spoke languages of their own. The simplest hypothesis under the circumstances is, as Tamil traditional history holds, that the Tamils and other allied peoples were indigenous and their languages were evolved where they are now spoken''
(Stone Age in India by P.T. Srinivasa Iyengar, pp. 46-7)
pērā. vi. ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar āṅkilattil eḻutiya `varalāṟṟu muṉṉait teṉṉintiyā' (Pre-Historic South India), tamiḻar tōṟṟamum paravalum' (Origin and Spread of the Tamils) eṉṉum iru varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ, tamiḻar kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟip pāreṅkum paraviyatai naṭunilaiyākavum maṟuppiṟ kiṭamiṉṟiyum viḷakkik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
vaṭanūlkaḷuḷ, catapata pirāmaṇam, oru teṉticaik kaṭal kōḷukkut tappiya pāṇṭiyaṉait tiraviṭapatiyeṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
moḻiyiyaṟ cāṉṟukaḷ
1. tamiḻum ataṉ tiripāṉa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷum intiyāviṟ kuḷḷēyē vaḻaṅkutalum, avaṟṟuḷ ilakkiyamuḷḷa mutaṉmaiyāṉa nāṉmoḻiyum teṉṉintiyāviṟkuḷḷēyē vaḻaṅkutalum, avaṟṟuḷ ḷum talaimaiyāṉa tamiḻ teṉkōṭiyil vaḻaṅkutalum, ataṉ vaḻakkum teṟkiṟ ciṟantiruttalum, vaṭakkē cellac cella ēṉait tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ ilakkaṇamiṉṟiyum ilakkiya miṉṟiyum paṇpāṭiṉṟiyum colvaḷamiṉṟiyum koccaiyākiyum mēṉmēlum ciṟuttum citaintum kalavaiyākiyum pōvatum, intiyāviṟku veḷiyē citainta tamiḻ allatu tiraviṭac coṟkaḷaṉṟi moḻiyētum vaḻaṅkāmaiyum.
2. intiyiṉ aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ citainta teṉ collā yiruttal.
mutal karupporuṭ cāṉṟukaḷ
1. kāla vēṟupāṭum iyaṟkai nilaimaikaḷum uyiriṉa vakaikaḷum viḷaivup poruḷkaḷum toṉṟutoṭṭu māṟātiruntu varutal.
2. cilappatikārattil collappaṭṭuḷḷa kārōtimam (kāraṉṉam) iṉṟu āttirēliyāvilum tācumēṉiyāvilum maṭṭum iruttalum, teṉṟicaip peyarukkuk kāraṇamāṉa teṉṉaimaram teṉkiḻakkut tīvukaḷil maṭṭum iyaṟkaiyāka vaḷartalum.
maṟuppiṟku maṟuppu
mēṉāṭṭu iṭappeyarīṟukaṭkut tamiḻilēyē poruḷiruttalālum, kumari nāṭṭiliruntu naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkarai nāṭṭiṟku mutaṟkaṇ ceṉṟa makkaḷ nīḷmaṇṭaiyarā yiruntataṉālum, ūr eṉṉum marutanilak kuṭiyiruppuppeyar kumarināṭṭilēyē tōṉṟiyamaiyālum, paruṅkaṟ kaṭṭaṭa amaippē ciṟukaṟ kaṭṭaṭa amaippiṟku muntuvatu iyaṟkaiyāta lālum, ilākōvāri (Lahovary), pālakiruṭṭiṇaṉ nāyar mutaliyōr tamiḻiṉ allatu tamiḻariṉ toṉmaiyai maṟuppatu aṟiyāmai paṟṟiyatē yeṉṟu kūṟi viṭukka.
muṭinta muṭipu
tamiḻ, teṉṉāṭṭiṟkum teṟkil teṉmāvāriyil muḻukik kiṭakkum teṉkaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭi nāṭāṉa teṉpāliyil tōṉṟiya teṉmoḻiyē. ataṉāl, atait tōṟṟi vaḷartta tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamum at teṉpulamē.
iv vaṭippaṭaiyilēyē, tamiḻiṉ amaippum ataṉ tiraviṭat tiripum āriya vēṟupāṭum teḷivāṉa viḷakkam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. iv vuṇmaiyai aṟiyātār allatu uṇarātār tamiḻiṉ iyalpai maṭṭumaṉṟi oppiyaṉ moḻinūlaiyum aṟiyātavarē yāvar.

tamiḻaṉ urimai vēṭkai
urimai eṉṟāl eṉṉa?
māntaṉ iv vulakil irukkai, kālantaḷḷukaiyum vāḻkkaiyum vāḻvum eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. varumāṉam, nukarporuḷ, uṭalnalam mutaliyaṉa kuṉṟiyiruttal kālantaḷḷukai; avai iṉṟiyamaiyāta aḷaviruttal vāḻkkai ; avai aḷaviṟantiruttal vāḻvu. ivaṟṟaip pulvāḻkkai, aḷavu vāḻkkai, nalvāḻkkai eṉṟuṅ kūṟalām.
vāḻvum, urimai vāḻvu, aṭimai vāḻvu eṉa iruvakaippaṭum.
taṉ illattiṟ kuṭiyiruntu, uṭaluḻaippālō maṉavuḻaippālō tāṉē tēṭiya poruḷaik koṇṭu, iṟaivaṉaṭiyār, viruntāḷar, ēḻaiyuṟaviṉar, irappōr, pulavar ākiya aivakuppārkku aḷittuṇṭu, taṉ tāymoḻi ilakkiyac cemmoḻiyāyiṉ atil iyaṉṟavarai tūymaiyākap pēciyeḻuti, kaṭavuḷ nampikkaiyiruppiṉ taṉ tāy moḻiyilēyē vaḻipaṭṭu, taṉ muṉṉōr vaḷartta paṇpāṭṭaip pōṟṟikkāttu, pakuttaṟivōṭum taṉmāṉattōṭum vāḻvatē, taṉimakaṉ urimai vāḻvu. tam nāṭṭil allatu tamakkeṉṟuriya oru taṉinilap pakutiyil vatintu, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu vāḻntu varuvatē oru nāṭṭiṉattiṉ allatu makkaḷ vakuppiṉ urimai vāḻvu.
tammil iruntu tamatupāt tuṇṭaṟṟāl	(kuṟaḷ. 1107)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟutal kāṇka.
kalvikkum ayalnāṭṭuṟaviṟkum oru moḻiyait terintu koḷvatum, urimaiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatē.
mūvakai vāḻkkaiyum, urimaiyiṉṟēl aṭimai vāḻkkaiyē.
tamiḻaṉukku urimaiyuṇṭā?
tamiḻaṉ eṉṟatu iṟṟait tamiḻaṉai.
oru vīṭṭiṟku āvaṇam pōl, oru nāṭṭiṟku urimaic cāṉṟu uṇmaiyāṉa varalāṟu. inta nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattilēyē, maṉōṉmaṇīya āciriyar cuntarampiḷḷai, intiyā cariyāṉapaṭi teṟkiluḷḷateṉṟum, intiya varalāṟṟait teṟkiṉiṉṟē toṭaṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟum, eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭār. intiya varalāṟṟut tantaiyeṉat takum viṉceṉṟu cimitum, tam muntukāla intiyā (Early India) eṉṉum varalāṟṟup pottakattil, cuntaram piḷḷaiyiṉ kūṟṟaip pārāṭṭi, etirkāla intiya varalāṟṟu āciriyarellārum tam varalāṟṟu nūlait teṟkiṉiṉṟē toṭaṅka vēṇṭumeṉṟu valuvuṟac cārnturaittār.
ak koḷkaiyait taḻuvi, pi.ti.cīnivācaiyaṅkār intiyak kaṟkālam (Stone Age In India), āriyarkku muṉṉait tamiḻk kalai nākarikam (Pre-Aryan Tamil Culture), tamiḻar varalāṟu (History of the Tamils) mutaliya nūlkaḷaiyum; vi.rā. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar tamiḻar tōṟṟamum paravalum (Origin and Spread of the Tamils), varalāṟṟiṟku muṉṉait teṉṉintiyā (Pre-Historic South India) mutaliya nūlkaḷaiyum, evarum maṟukka muṭiyātavāṟu tiṟampaṭa eḻutip pōyiṉar.
āyiṉ, avar maṟaintapiṉ, pērā. kē. nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyār avar koḷkaikkuṅ kūṟṟiṟkum nērmāṟāka, āriya vētattaiyē aṭippaṭai yākak koṇṭu intiya varalāṟṟai vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki, uṇmaik kum uttikkum ovvāvāṟu pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉaip peṟṟāṉ eṉpatupōl, tamiḻar varalāṟṟait talaikīḻāka varaintuḷḷār.
tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam teṟkil muḻukip pōṉa kumarināṭeṉṉum aṭippaṭaiyilēyē, tamiḻiṉ ciṟappaiyum tamiḻaṉ uyarvaiyum, uṇmaiyāka uṇartaloṇṇum.
varalāṟṟiṟku muṉṉait teṉṉintiyā eṉṉum varalāṟṟup pottakam ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka veḷiyīṭāyiruntum, ataip paṭiyāmaiyālō, tam iyaṟkaiyāṉa aṭimaittaṉattālō, vaiyāpurit taṉmaiyālō, talaimaip patavit tamiḻp pērāciriyarum pērāciriyar nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyārai etirkkat tuṇivatillai. ataṉāl, tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyum, muṉmaiyum, tāymaiyum, talaimaiyum tamiḻnāṭṭilum oppukkoḷḷappaṭāmalē yirukkiṉṟaṉa.
oru moḻiyiṉ ciṟappai eṭuttukkāṭṭa, akaramutalipōṟ ciṟantatu vēṟoṉṟumillai. ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali (Lexicon) 23 āṇṭukaḷākap potumakkaḷ paṇattil nālaraiyilakkam urūpā celaviṭṭut tokukkappaṭṭatu. āyiṉ, mūla (āti) vaiyā puriyārait tuṇaikkoṇṭu tamiḻp pakaivarāṉa pirāmaṇat tamiḻp pulavarē atait tokuttataṉāl, tamiḻ oru puṉciṟu putukkalavai moḻiyeṉak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. av vakaramutaliyiṉ kuṟṟaṅkuṟaikaḷai yellān teḷḷat teḷivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyum, taṉṉalap piṇṭaṅ kaḷāṉa talaimait tamiḻp pērāciriyaruṭ cilarum ataip puṟakkaṇiyātu pōṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam, tamiḻ varalāṟu maṟaikkappaṭṭum, maṟukkappaṭṭum, tamiḻmoḻi tāḻttappaṭṭum vīḻttappaṭṭum iruppatāl, tamiḻ aṉaittintiya āṭci moḻiyākātum, tirukkōvil vaḻipāṭu tamiḻil naṭaipeṟātum pōkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl tamiḻ vaḷarcciyum tamiḻaṉ muṉṉēṟṟamum taṭaippaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ovvoru nāṭṭilum makkaḷ muṉṉēṟum vaḻi moḻiyē. atu tāymoḻiyākavum irukkalām; ayaṉ moḻiyākavum irukkalām. tāymoḻiyil muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkēṟṟa ilakkiyamillāviṭiṉ, aḵtuḷḷa ayaṉmoḻiyē muṉṉēṟṟa vaḻiyākum. itaṟku, āppirikka ātti rēliya amerikkap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷē talaiciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭāvar.
tamiḻil ōraḷavu paṇṭaiyilakkiyam iruppiṉum, ikkālak kalvi aṟiviyamum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa nuṇkammiyamumē yātalāl, makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku āṅkilam pōṉṟa oru mēlai moḻiyaṟivu iṉṟiyamaiyātatākiṉṟatu. ataṉāl, atu ayalārāl valintu pukuttap paṭātu makkaḷālēyē virumpik kaṟkappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu aṭimait taṉamaṉṟu.
āyiṉ, ulakil vēṟeṅkaṇum kāṇamuṭiyāta oru vēṭikkaiyum viyappumāṉa moḻiyaṭimaittaṉam, in nāvalan tēyattil, ciṟappākat tamiḻnāṭṭil, kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭāka iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. atu vētamoḻiyum camaṟkirutamum tēvamoḻiyeṉpatum, atai ilakkiya vaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkuvōr nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉpatum, atiṉiṉṟu ēṉai moḻikaḷellām kaṭaṉ koḷḷumeṉpatum, atu maṭṭum vēṟem moḻiyiṉiṉṟum kaṭaṉ koḷḷāteṉpatum, atu pirāmaṇaṉālēyē cariyākap palukkappaṭum (uccarikkappaṭum) eṉpatum, ataṉ palukkam immi tiṟampiṉum, peruṅkēṭu viḷaiyum eṉpatum, atiṟ ceyyappaṭum vaḻipāṭē iṟaivaṉukkup pēriṉpan taruvateṉpatum, pirāmaṇaṉukkut toṇṭu ceyvatē aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar, kaittoḻilāḷar ākiya piṟavakuppārellām uyyum vaḻi yeṉpatum, piṟavumām.
iv vulakil toṉṟutoṭṭu vāḻntu varupavar makkaḷē. ātalāl, ulakamoḻikaḷ ellām makkaḷ moḻikaḷē. vēta moḻiyō camaṟ kirutamō tēvamoḻiyāyiṉ, avaṟṟōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuṟṟa kirēkka moḻiyum, ataṟkiṉamāṉa ilattīṉam, cerumāṉiyam mutaliya airōppiya moḻikaḷum tēvamoḻikaḷā yiruttal vēṇṭum. avai aṅṅaṉam illai.
pirāmaṇariṉ muṉṉōr veṇṇiṟattarā yiruntataṉāl tēva reṉap paṭṭāreṉiṉ, iṉṟu avar vaḻiyiṉar. an niṟattarāyillai. iṉṟu airōppiyarum avar vaḻiyiṉarumē nilattēvar eṉappaṭal vēṇṭum.
vētam eṉṉum maṟainūlāl āriyam tēvamoḻiyāyiṟṟeṉiṉ ciṟuteyva vaḻuttut tiraṭṭākiya vētattiṉum vīṭupēṟaḷikkum perun tēva vaḻuttut tiraṭṭum kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttut tiraṭṭumākiya tamiḻmaṟaikaḷ, kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa maṭaṅku ciṟantaṉavātal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, vētamoḻi vallōcaiyum mūccoliyum nirampiyataṉāl tēvamoḻi yeṉappaṭum eṉiṉ, av viraṇṭum paṉmaṭaṅku ciṟanta kāḷavāyk kattalum tēvamoḻik kiṉamātal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, vētamoḻiyum camaṟkirutamum ettakaiya moḻikaḷ eṉpataip pārppōm.
vētamoḻi eṉpatu ettakaiyatu?
vēta āriyar mikac ciṟupāṉmaiyarā yiruntataṉāl, kirēkkattiṟku mika nerukkamāyirunta avar moḻi, akkālattu vaṭanāṭṭu vaṭṭāra moḻiyākiya pirākirutattoṭu iraṇṭaṟak kalantu, ataṉ pāṉmaiyatāki ekara okarak kuṟil iḻantu irumoḻik kalavaiyāyiṟṟu.
camaṟkirutam ettakaiyatu?
tamiḻiluḷḷa paltuṟai yilakkiyattai moḻipeyarttaṟku vēta moḻi pōtiya colvaḷamiṉṟi yiruntataṉāl, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻc coṟkaḷai vētamoḻiyōṭu kalantu, camaṟkirutam eṉṉum araic ceyaṟkai yilakkiya moḻiyai uṇṭākkiṉar.
camaṟkirutam (samskruta) eṉṟālē kalantu ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉpatutāṉ poruḷ.
kummutal = kūṭutal, kalattal. kum - kummal = kumpal, kuviyal.    L. cumulus, heap. itiṉiṉṟē cumulate, accumulate mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
kum - kummi = kai kuvittaṭittup pāṭiyāṭum kūttu.
kum - kumi - kuvi. kumi - kumiḻ - kumiḻi = kuvinta nīr moṭṭu. kuvi - kuviyal, kuvāl, kuvai.
kum - kumpu - kumpal. kumpu - kuppu - kuppal, kuppai, kuppam, koppam.
kumpu - kūmpu - kūppu, kūmputal = kuvital.
kai kūpputal = kai kuvittal.
kumpu - kumpiṭu, kumpiṭutal = kai kuvittal.
kum - kom - kommai. kommai koṭṭutal = kai taṭṭi yaḻaittal.
kummi - kommi = kummiyāṭṭam.
kommi - te. koppi (gobbi).
Cum (kum) eṉpatu ilattīṉ moḻiyil oru kūṭṭiṇaippuc col (Cumulative Conjunction). atu āṅkilattil com, con, col, cor, co eṉṟu tirintu with, together, altogether, completely eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muṉṉoṭṭu (prefix) ākum.
atuvē kirēkka moḻiyil sym, syn, syl, sy eṉṟu tirintu together, together with, in union with eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muṉṉoṭṭākum.
atuvē camaṟkirutattil sam eṉṟu tirintu, with, together with, along with, together, altogether eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muṉṉoṭṭākum.
samskruta eṉṉum colliṟku, put together, constructed, well or completely formed, perfected eṉṟu māṉiyar uvilliyamcu camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akaramutali poruḷ kūṟutal kāṇka.
karuttal eṉpatu, ceytal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭṭuk kaṭṭal (kaḷavu ceytal), viṉṉal (viṉavutal) eṉpaṉa pōlak kumarināṭṭil vaḻaṅkip piṉṉar vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉa oru tūya teṉcol.
tamiḻakattil toṉṟutoṭṭu veḷḷāḷar, kārāḷar eṉṟu iruvēṟu niṟattār iruntuvantirukkiṉṟaṉar. aḷaviṟantu veyiliṟ kāypavar karuttiruttalum, niḻalil vēlai ceypavar veḷuttiruttalum perumpāṉmai yiyalpu. kaiyiṟ kāḻppēṟa vēlaiceyyiṉ karuppar kai karuttum, civappar kai civantum pōkum. ataṉāl, ceytalaik kuṟikkak karuttal ceytal, eṉa iru viṉaiccoṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ceyta kai cēvēṟum ceyyāta kai nōvēṟum eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
karu - karumam - kammam = kammiyat toḻil.
kammañcey mākkaḷ	(nālaṭi. 393)
kammam - kam - kammavar - kammavāru (te.) = mutal toḻilāṉa uḻavaic ceyyum teluṅka vakuppār.
karumam - (va.) karmaṉ
kam = 1. toḻil
	īmuṅ kammum urumeṉ kiḷaviyum (tol. eḻuttu. 33)

	kam - kām (inti)
kam - kammāḷaṉ = 1. kollaṉ, taccaṉ, kaṟṟaccaṉ, taṭṭāṉ, kaṉṉāṉ eṉṉum aivakaik kollaruḷ oru vakuppāṉ. 2. poṟkollaṉ.
kammāḷaṉ - pirā. kammāra, (va.) kārmāra.
kam - kammi = 1. toḻilāḷi, maṭkalañcey kammi (pārata. tirau. 64) 2. kaṟṟaccaṉ.
kam - kammiyam = 1. kaittoḻil, 2. kammāḷattoḻil, 3. kaṭṭaṭattoḻil (va.) karmaṇya.
kammiyaṉ = 1. toḻilāḷi kammiyarum ūrvar kaḷiṟu (cīvaka. 495) 2. kammāḷaṉ 3. kaṟṟaccu.
kammiya nūl = kaṭṭaṭa nūl (ciṟpa cāstiram) ``kammiyanūṟ ṟolvaram pellai kaṇṭu (tiruviḷai. tirunakaraṅ. 38)
kammakkuṭam = kaṉṉāṉ ceyta kuṭam (naṉ. 222, mayilai).
kammakārar = 1. kalattaccar 2. kappalōṭṭikaḷ. kammakārar koṇṭāṭumpaṭi naṉṟāka ōṭiṟṟu (cīvaka. 501, urai)
karu eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyaik kru eṉṟu tirippar vaṭamoḻi yāḷar.
o.nō. : turu (turuvu) - (va.) tru. peru - (va.) pruh.
poṟu - (va.) pru (bhru).
cey = civappu. ceyya = civanta ceyyaṉ = civantavaṉ, civaṉ.
karuttal, ceytal eṉṉum iraṇṭum uṟaviyaṟ coṟkaḷ (Relative terms)
karu - karuvi = karumañ ceyvataṟ kētuvāṉa tuṇaipporuḷ. ic col vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
karu + aṇam = karaṇam = 1. ceykai. 2. karuvi. ic colliṉiṉṟu kāraṇa, kārya eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷaip piṟappittuḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ivai mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟilumillai.
karuttal, ceytal eṉṉum iruviṉaikaḷum, veḷuttal (calavai ceytal) eṉpatupōla, taṉviṉaiyākavum piṟaviṉaiyākavum āḷappeṟṟaṉa.
camaṟkirutam eṉṉum moḻic ciṟappup peyar maṭṭumaṉṟip pāṣai (pāṣā) eṉṉum potuppeyarum teṉcol tiripē.
oruvar vāyiṉiṉṟu coṟkaḷ varuvatu, oru tuḷaiyiṉiṉṟu nīr oḻukuvatum vāṉattiṉiṉṟu maḻai peyvatum pōṉṟirukkiṟatu. nīroḻukkuc ciṟuttiruppiṉ peytal eṉṟum, tiraṇṭiruppiṉ poḻital eṉṟum collappaṭum. aṅṅaṉamē, oruvar kaṭalmaṭai tiṟantāṟpōl taṭṭuttaṭaiyiṉṟip pēciṉ, kōṭai maḻaipōṟ poḻikiṟār eṉṟu colvatu vaḻakkam. atupaṟṟiyē coṟpoḻivu eṉṉum kūṭṭuc collum tōṉṟiṟṟu. ākavē, viraintu pēcuvatu poḻivatum viraiviṉṟip pēcuvatu peyvatum ākum eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.
peytal eṉṟa col koccai vaḻakkil pēytal eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum. ataṉāl, pēyntuṅ keṭṭatu, ōyntuṅ keṭṭatu eṉṉum paḻamoḻi eḻuntatu.
pēy - pēyu - pēcu - pēccu.
pēcu - (va.) pāṣ (bhash) - pāṣā
o.nō. : tēy-tēyu-tēcu
(va.) tējas. tēcu - (i.) tēj.
koccai vaḻakkiṉiṉṟum ceñcol allatu ilakkaṇac col piṟappatuṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	koṇṭuvā - koṇṭā - koṇā - koṇar. koṇṭā eṉpatu koccaic collākavum, koṇā, koṇar eṉpaṉa ceñcollākavum, koḷḷappaṭutal kāṇka.
iṉi, āriya mutaṉūlāṉatum, iṟaivaṉālum iyaṟṟappaṭātu eṉṟumuḷḷatākac collappaṭuvatumāṉa vētattiṉ peyarē, teṉ colliṉ tiripāyiruppatu, ciṟappākak kavaṉikkat takkatum mika makiḻcci taruvatumākum.
viḻittal = 1. kaṇtiṟattal, 2. kavaṉittup pārttal, 3. aṟital,           4. viḷaṅkutal, 5. teḷital.
viḻi = 1. kaṇ 2. aṟivam (ñāṉam). tēṟār viḻiyilā māntar (tiruman. 177)
viḻi - L. vide, Gk. (w)eid. OE. wit, Skt. vit.
vit - vēta = aṟivu, aṟivu nūl, maṟai.
ceviyuṟutal = kēṭṭal.

muṉṉutal = karututal, AS. mun (to think)
muṉṉam = 1. karuttu, 2 karutum maṉam (tivā.).
muṉ - muṉṉam - muṉam - maṉam. (va.) maṉas. L. mens, E.mind.
maṉ + tiram = mantiram = 1. karutuntiṟam 2. (tiṇṇiya) eṇṇam (piṅ.) 3. cūḻvu, 4. niṟaimoḻiyāṉa maṟaimoḻi. (tol. cey, 176). 5. tirumūlar tirumantiram 6. pēyviṉai maṟaimoḻi. (va.) mantra.

mantiram - (va.) mantira
maṇṭutal = vaḷaital, maṇṭiyiṭutal = kālai maṭakkutal, maṇṭu - maṇṭalam = 1. vaṭṭam, 2. vaṭṭāram 3. nilappakuti, 4. kālappakuti (40 allatu 48 nāḷ) 5. nūṟpakuti 6. vētanūṟ pakuti.
maṇṭutal = 1. neruṅkutal, 2. kūṭutal, 3 niṟaital. maṇṭu - maṇṭakam = makkaḷ kūṭum kūṭam.
maṇṭakappaṭi eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.

o.nō. vāṇikam - vāṇipam. maṇṭapam - (va) maṇṭapa.
muṉ - maṉ = ciṟappākak karutiyaṟiyum māntaṉ, maṉpatai = makkaṭ kūṭṭam.
OE. man, OS., OHG, man, Goth. manna,
Skt. maṉu - maṉuṣya - (maṉuṭaṉ, māṉiṭaṉ, maṉitaṉ)
iṅṅaṉam, camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷuḷ ainti lirupakuti tamiḻ. camaṟkirutattilum ēṉaiyāriya moḻikaḷilum uḷḷa cuṭṭuc coṟkaṭ kellām mūlam, a,i,u ākiya tamiḻ muccuṭṭukaḷē.
kiṟittuviṟku muṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē, āriyap pūcāriyar tammai nilattēvareṉṟum tam ilakkiya moḻiyait tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum colli, palvēḷviccālai mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti, irāca cūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷi, palyāṉaic celkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉ pōṉṟa mūvēntarai yum ēmāṟṟi, ākamaṅkaḷai eḻuti vaittukkoṇṭaṉar. avai aṟivārāycci mikka ikkālattiṟ cellā.
nāṭu tamiḻnāṭu. makkaḷ tamiḻmakkaḷ. matam tamiḻar matam. kōvil tamiḻaracar allatu tiraviṭa aracar allatu tamiḻ nāṭṭaracar kaṭṭiyavai. avaṟṟiluḷḷa teyvap paṭimaikaḷ tamiḻak kammiyar cetukkiyavai. ennāṭṭilum tāymoḻiyil vaḻipāṭu ceyvatē, iyalpum muṟaimaiyum payaṉpaṭuvatum iṟaivaṉukku ēṟpumākum. camaṟkirutam ayaṉmoḻiyum, perumpālum tamiḻk kalappā lāṉatum, tamiḻai oḻikka vēṇṭumeṉṟē puṉaiyappaṭṭuk karavākap pukuttappaṭṭatum, eṅkēṉum eṉṟēṉum vaḻaṅkātu ilakkiya moḻiyākavē yiruntu varuvatum, tamiḻarkku immiyum poruḷ teriyātatum, āriyap pūcāriyarkkum teḷivāy viḷaṅkātatum, tamiḻar muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku valutta muṭṭukkaṭṭaiyāṉatum, tamiḻiṉa oṟṟumaiyaik kulaippatum, avaratu aṭimaittaṉattai nilainiṟuttuvatumāṉa pōlimoḻi yātalāl, tirukkōvil tamiḻ vaḻipāṭṭai vēṭpatum, ataṟkēṟṟa caṭṭam iyaṟṟuvatum, iṟaivaṉum etirkkavoṇṇāp piṟappurimaiyē. atai maṟuttaṟku oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ (U.N.) cārpāṉa ulakat tīrppu maṉṟattiṟkum atikāramillai.
tamiḻ viṭutalaiyē tamiḻaṉ viṭutalai. tamiḻaṉ iṉṉum viṭutalai peṟavillai.
tamiḻar, tām kumarināṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ vaḻiyiṉar eṉṉum varalāṟṟuṇmaiyaṟintu, muḻuvurimaiyum viraintu peṟuka.
- ``urimai vēṭkai'' poṅkal malar 1975
19
urimaippēṟu
oruvarkku uriyatu urimai. atu puṟavuṭal paṟṟiyatum akavuṭal paṟṟiyatum āka irutiṟappaṭum. muṉṉatu ellārkkum potu; piṉṉatu paṇpaṭṭa makkaṭkē ciṟappu. māntarkku ovvoru nilaiyilum ovvoru allatu ciṟcila urimaikaḷ tēvai. avai nīrvēṭkaiyum uṇavu vēṭkaiyum pōṉṟavai. avaṟṟai muyaṉṟō, piṟarai vēṇṭiyō, avarōṭu pōrāṭiyō, peṟal vēṇṭum.
irutiṟa vurimaikaḷum piṉvarumāṟu aivakaippaṭum.
1. vaḷarvurimai
kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyariṉ iṉpak kūṭṭattōṭu piḷḷaippēṟu toṭarpupaṭuttappaṭṭiruppatāl, peṟṟōr virumpiṉum virumpā viṭiṉum perumpālum piḷḷaikaḷ piṟantuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. nilattil vitaitta vitai muḷaittal pōṉṟē piḷḷaippēṟum iruppatāl, 
kēḷātē vantu kiḷaikaḷā yiṟṟōṉṟi	(30)
eṉṟu nālaṭiyār kūṟuvatu poruntātu. piḷḷaikaḷ peṟṟōr viṉaiyālēyē peṟappaṭuvatāl, avaṟṟai vaḷarppatu avar kaṭamaiyum, avarāl vaḷarkkappaṭuvatu avaṟṟiṉ urimaiyum, ākiṉṟatu.
emmaik kēḷātu ēṉ piṟantāy?
eṉṟu, peṟṟōr tam piḷḷai vaḷarppait taṭṭik kaḻittuviṭa muṭiyātu.
kuḻavi piṟantavuṭaṉ uṇaviṟku aḻuvatē, ōr urimai vēṭkai yum ataip peṟum muyaṟciyum ākiṉṟatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil tāy vīṭṭu vēlaiyil āḻntirukkumpōtu, uriya vēḷaiyil taṉ cēykkup pāl koṭātiruppatum uṇṭu. av vēḷaiyil kuḻantaiyiṉ aḻukaiyoli kēṭṭut tāy pāl koṭuppatu vaḻakkam. ataṉāl, aḻutapiḷḷai pāl kuṭikkum eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum eḻuntatu.
kuḻavip paruvattilum kuḻantaip paruvattilum piḷḷaip paruvattilum vaḷarppatum, paiyaṟ paruvattilum iḷamaṭṭap paruvattilum uṇavaḷittuk kāppatōṭu ōr aluvaṟkō toḻilukkō payiṟṟu vippatum, paruvam vantapiṉ maṇañceytu vaippatum, peṟṟōriṉ kaṭamaiyām. annaṉmaikaḷaip peṟuvatu piḷḷaikaḷiṉ urimaiyām.
kaittoḻiṟ payiṟciyiṉuṅ kalvip payiṟci ciṟantatātalālum, iruvakaip payiṟciyum oruṅkē peṟutaluṅ kūṭumātalālum,
tantai makaṟkāṟṟum naṉṟi avaiyattu
munti yiruppac ceyal.	(kuṟaḷ. 67)
īṉṟa poḻutiṟ perituvakkum taṉmakaṉaic
cāṉṟōṉ eṉakkēṭṭa tāy	(kuṟaḷ. 69)
eṉṟu tiruvāymalarntār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
2. vāḻvurimai
oruvar iv vulakil vāḻvataṟkuk kalvip payiṟci allatu toḻiṟ payiṟci, vēlaippēṟu, pātukāppu - im mūṉṟum iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa. oru kuṭumpattiṟ piṟantavaṉukku eṅṅaṉam vaḷarvurimaiyuṇṭō, aṅṅaṉamē oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟantavaṉukku vāḻvurimaiyuṇṭu. ataṟkuriya mūṉṟaiyum aḷikkak kūṭiyatum kaṭamaippaṭṭuḷḷatum, āḷum aracē.
payiṟciyiṉṟi evvēlaiyum peṟavum  ceyyavum iyalātākai yāl, nāṭṭiṟ piṟantavarkkellām, avaravar tiṟamai, viruppam, uṭaṟkūṟu, maṉappāṉmai, mativaṉmai mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkut takkavāṟu, vevvēṟu tuṟaiyiṟ payiṟciyaḷittal vēṇṭum. eṇṇum eḻuttum kaṇṇeṉat takum. ātalāl, mutaṟkaṇ, nālakavaikku mēṟpaṭṭa ellāp piḷḷai kaṭkum ilavacak kaṭṭāyat tuvakkak kalviyaḷittal vēṇṭum. enta amaippum nilaiyākavum uṟutiyākavum iruttaṟku aṭippaṭai akaṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl, mēṟpaṭai kulaintupōm.
viḷaiyum payir muḷaiyil teriyum.'' ātalāl, tuvakkappaḷḷik kalvip paruvattilēyē, peṟṟōrum āciriyarum piḷḷaikaḷiṉ tiṟamaiyaiyum maṉappāṉmaiyaiyum kūrntu nōkkiyaṟintu, akakkaraṇa valimai viñciyavarai mēṟkalvip payiṟcikkum, puṟakkaraṇa valimai viñciyavarait toḻiṟpayiṟcikkum, aṉupputal vēṇṭum.
mēṟkalvip payiṟciyilum, aṉpum toṇṭu maṉappāṉmaiyum uḷḷavarai maruttuvak kalvikkum nāvaṉmaiyum niṉaivāṟṟalum naṭunilaiyum uḷḷavaraic caṭṭak kalvikkum, ōviya varaivāṟṟalum putup puṉaivut tiṟaṉum kaṇita matiyum uḷḷavaraip poṟiviṉai allatu cūḻcciyaviṉaik kalvikkum, iṭaiviṭāp paṭippum eṭuttu viḷakkun tiṟaṉum uḷḷavarai āciriyap payiṟcik kalvikkum, vaḷarttiyum uṭal valimaiyum añcāmaiyum tuṇiceyal maṟamum uḷḷavaraip paṭait tuṟaik kalvikkum, iṉkuralum icaiviruppum nīḷviralum uḷḷavarai icaikkalvikkum, teyvappattiyum tūyavoḻukkamum maṟainūl viruppum uḷḷavarai mataviyaṟ kalvikkum, ivvāṟē piṟa tuṟaikaṭkum terinteṭuttal vēṇṭum.
kallūrikaḷil iṭamillāviṭiṉ, putukkallūrikaḷait tōṟṟu vittal vēṇṭum. kāraik kaṭṭaṭam illāviṭiṉ kūraik kaṭṭaṭatti lēṉum toṭaṅkutal vēṇṭum.
kaṇitam vēṇṭiyavaṉai varalāṟṟu vakuppilum, maruttuvap payiṟci virumpupavaṉaic caṭṭak kallūriyilum, iṅṅaṉamē ēṉait tuṟaikaḷilum tāṟumāṟākac cērppatu, vaṇṭiyiṟ kaṭṭavēṇṭiya kāḷaiyai vīṭṭuk kāvaṟkum, vīṭṭuk kāvaṟkuriya nāyai iṟaivaiyēṟṟattiṟkum, poti cumakka vēṇṭiya kaḻutaiyai ūrvalattiṟkum, ūrvalattiṟkuriya yāṉaiyai narakal vaṇṭikkum, vaippatum kaṭṭuvatum amarttuvatum pōṉṟatē.
toḻiṟpayiṟciyāyiṉum mēṟkalvip payiṟciyāyiṉum, payiṟci peṟṟa ellārum vēlaikkuriyar. palarukku vēlaiyillai yeṉṟu kaivirippatu poṟuppāṭciyaṉṟu. makkaḷtokai mikkiruppiṉ, ataip palvēṟu vaḻikaḷil maṭṭuppaṭuttalām. āyiṉ,
irantum uyirvāḻtal vēṇṭiṉ parantu
keṭuka ulakiyaṟṟi yāṉ	(kuṟaḷ. 1062)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar iṟaivaṉ mītē vacai pāṭiyiruttalai nōkkuka.
makkaḷtokai maṭṭiṟku miñciya ikkālattil, taṉiyuṭaimai yāṭcikku iṭamiṉṟēl, aḷavuṭaimai yāṭciyum, atuvum pōtā teṉiṉ kūṭṭuṭaimai yāṭciyum, iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavām. oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟantu vēlaip payiṟci peṟṟa ellārkkum kulamatak kaṭci vēṟu pāṭiṉṟit tiṟamaikkēṟṟavāṟu vēlaiyaḷippatē kūṭṭuṭaimaiyāṭci yām. atuvē iraciyāvilum cīṉāvilum iṉṟu iruntuvaruvatu. ataip potuvuṭaimaiyāṭciyeṉpatu tavaṟu. potuvuṭamaiyāṭci paṇamiṉṟik kuṭumpa vāḻkkaipōl naṭappatu. atu oru nāṭṭiṟku ēṟkātu.
pakuttuṇṭu palluyir ōmputal nūlōr
tokuttavaṟṟuḷ ellān talai	(kuṟaḷ. 302)
eṉṟu akkālattilēyē tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
aḷavuṭaimaiyāṭci yeṉiṉum varampuṭaimaiyāṭci yeṉiṉum okkum.
iṉi, kuruṭar muṭavar mutaliyōr aiyam (piccai) peṟuvatum, vaṟiya nāṭukaḷ celva nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu utavi peṟuvatum vāḻvurimaiyiṉpāṟ paṭuvatē,
3. viṭutalaiyurimai
taṉippaṭṭa aṭimai taṉ talaivaṉiṭattiṉiṉṟum, paṇṇai yāḷkaḷ tam āṇṭaiyiṉiṉṟum, oru kulattār iṉṉoru kulattāri ṉiṉṟum, oru nāṭṭār iṉṉoru nāṭṭāriṉiṉṟum, viṭutalai peṟuvatu viṭutalaiyurimaiyām.
āṭavarpōṟ kalvi peṟuvatum tērtalkaḷiṟ kuṭavōlai yiṭuvatum, aluvalakaṅkaḷil āṭavaruṭaṉ vēlai ceyvatum, kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ kaimmai (kaimpeṇ) vāḻvu vāḻvatum maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷvatum, peṇṭir peṟa vēṇṭiya urimaikaḷām.
kulak kaṭṭuppāṭum peṟṟōr taṭaiyum mīṟic ciṟanta muṟaiyil ulakam pōṟṟumāṟu kūṭi vāḻvatu, maṇamakkaḷ urimaiyām.
iṉi, kaṭcit talaivarkkuriyatu pēccurimai; eḻuttāḷarukkum nūlāciriyarukkum uriyatu eḻutturimai; oruvaṉ taṉ tāymoḻiyiṟ pēca iṭamiruppatu moḻiyurimai; oru vakuppār oruvakai uṇavaiyuṇṇac caṭṭat taṭaiyillātiruppatu uṇavurimai.
iṉi, tamiḻ tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyum āriyattiṟku mūlamumāṉa ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyātalāl aṉaittintiya āṭci moḻiyākat tamiḻum amaivatu tamiḻariṉ moḻiyurimaiyām.
4. potunalavurimai
taṉi vāḻkkaiyil oruvaṉ evvaḷavum īṭṭalām; eṅkuñ cella lām. āyiṉ, kūṭṭu vāḻkkaiyil, potunalampaṟṟip paṟpala kaṭṭup pāṭukaḷum varampīṭukaḷum vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai taṉivāḻkkaiyil urimait taṭaiyēṉum, kūṭṭu vāḻkkaiyil urimaiyēyām.
iru piḷḷaikaṭkumēṟ peṟakkūṭāteṉpatu piḷḷai varampīṭu; patiṉaintu ceykaṭkumēl nilam vaittirukkak kūṭāteṉpatu, celva varampīṭṭil oruvakaiyāṉa nilavarampīṭu. ivaiyellām, oru mānakariṟ pakal vēḷaiyil pōkkuvarattu mikka iṭattil, oruvaṉ oru cālaiyiṉ kuṟukkē cellak kūṭāteṉpatu pōṉṟavaiyē.
5. matavurimai
ulakiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiyum karuttu vēṟu pāṭirukkumāṟu, makkaḷ maṉappāṉmai paltiṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ākavē, kāṇappaṭāta kaṭavuḷaip paṟṟiyum maṟumaiyaip paṟṟiyum karuttu vēṟu pāṭṭiṟku ettuṇai yiṭamuṇṭeṉpataic collavē vēṇṭuva tillai.
matanampikkaiyellām ēraṇa muṟaikkum aṟiviyal meyppiṟ kum aṭaṅkātu, appāṟpaṭṭu, ōraḷavu kuruṭṭuttaṉamākavō kuraṅkup piṭiyākavō iruppiṉum, uyirvāḻvaiyum poruṭpaṭuttāta aḷaviṟku makkaḷ maṉattai iṟukap piṇikkum ceytikaḷuḷ talaiciṟantatu matamātalāl, paṇpāṭu mikka ceṅkōlaracellām, tam kuṭikaṭku matavurimai yaḷittēyuḷḷaṉar.
nam intiyak kuṭiyaracu paṉṉāṭṭuk kūṭṭāṭciyātalāl, matavurimai yaḷittiruppatu mikat takkatē.
iṉi, civamatamum tirumāl matamum teṉṉāṭṭut tūya tamiḻ mataṅkaḷātalāl, iṉimēl tamiḻnāṭṭut tirukkōvilkaḷil, payiṟci peṟṟa palvakait tamiḻ vakuppārē, tamiḻil maṭṭum vaḻipāṭāṟṟutal vēṇṭum. itu tamiḻar matavurimaiyākum.
pirāmaṇar veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantēṟikaḷiṉ vaḻiyiṉarātalālum, ciṟupāṉmaiyarātalālum, tamiḻaiyē tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭiruppa tālum, āriyam tiraviṭattiṉ tiripātalālum, āriya mantiraṅkaḷellām tamiḻar aṟiyāta ciṟuteyvak kolaivēḷvi vaḻipāṭu paṟṟiyaṉavāta lālum, camaṟkirutam vētamoḻiyum tamiḻuṅ kalanta ilakkiya moḻi yātalālum, āriyamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum pirāmaṇar nilattēva reṉṟum ēmāṟṟappaṭṭataṉālēyē, iṭaikkālat tamiḻar pirāmaṇar āriya mantiraṅkaḷaik koṇṭu tirukkōvil vaḻipāṭu ceyya iṭantantaṉarātalālum, av vēmāṟṟu iṉṉun toṭara immiyun takutiyillai eṉa aṟika.
pirāmaṇar tamiḻukku uṇmaiyāyiruntu tamiḻilēyē vaḻipāṭu naṭattiṉ, avar pūcait toḻilait toṭarntu ceyyalām. itukāṟum uyartiṇaiyāṉa makkaḷiṉ palvēṟu urimaikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi, vāyillā ēḻai aḵṟiṇai uyiriṉaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ urimaiyai nām namatupōṟ pēṇa vēṇṭāvā? ataiyum pēṇā viṭiṉ, nām uyartiṇaiyeṉṟu tarukkuvatu eṅṅaṉ takum?
māṭukaḷum, kutiraikaḷum, ēruḻavum nīriṟaikkavum vaṇṭiyiḻukka vum, iravum pakalum vaṟpuṟuttappaṭṭu, pōtiya vuṇaviṉṟiyum muraṭṭut taṉamāka aṭiyuṇṭum kuttuṇṭum, puṇmēṟ puṇpaṭṭum ettuṇait tuṉpappaṭukiṉṟaṉa! avai eṅṅaṉ yāriṭam muṟaiyiṭum? eṅṅaṉam mānāṭu kūṭum? eṅṅaṉam kūṭṭaṇi cērum? eṅṅaṉam vēlai niṟuttañ ceyyum? uṇṇānōṉpiṉālum vaṉceyalālum tam tollaikaḷai yum tuṉpaṅkaḷaiyum terivikkavō tīrttuk koḷḷavō immiyum avaṟṟiṟku iṭamillaiyē!
makkaḷiṭattu aṉpu ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṟṟa vuyirkaḷiṭattu aruḷpūṇṭoḻuka vēṇṭumeṉṟum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa paṉuvalkaḷum nammiṭattuḷḷaṉa. iruntum eṉ payaṉ?
amerikkarum mēṉāṭṭārum nampōla naṭikkāviṭiṉum, poṟiviṉaip puraṭciyai uṇṭupaṇṇi ellā viṉaikaḷaiyum cūḻcciyat tuṇaikoṇṭē ceytuvaruvatāl, kutiraiyiṉamum kaḻutaiyiṉamum muṟṟum viṭutalai peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. māṭṭiṉam uṇaviṟkaṉṟi vēṟev vakaiyiluṅ kollappaṭuvatillai.
nāmum mēlaiyarpōṟ poṟiviṉaip puraṭciyai yuṇṭākki, mēṟkūṟiya vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷiṉ urimaiyaik kāppōmāka.
- ``urimai vēṭkai'' poṅkal malar 1973

-----
moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar 
vāḻkkaic cuvaṭukaḷ
ti.pi. 1933 (1902)	:	tirunelvēli māvaṭṭam, caṅkaranayiṉār kōyilil cuṟavam - 26ām nāḷ (7.2.1902) piṟantār. 
tantai	:	ñāṉamuttu
tāy	:	paripūraṇam
ti.pi. 1938 (1907)	:	vaṭārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam, āmpūr micauri
toṭakkakkalvi	:	nallañcal uluttariṉ viṭaiyūḻiya naṭunilaip paḷḷi.
uyarnilaikkalvi:	tirunelvēli māvaṭṭam, pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭai, tiruccapai viṭaiyūḻiya uyarnilaip paḷḷi (9,10,11 - vakuppu)
ti.pi. 1950-52(1919-21)	:	mukavai māvaṭṭam, tiruvilliputtūr vaṭṭam, cīyōṉ malaiyiluḷḷa cīyōṉ naṭunilaip paḷḷiyilmutaṟpaṭiva āciriyap paṇiyiṟ cērntār.
ti.pi. 1952-53(1921-22)	:	vaṭārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam, āmpūr naṭunilaip paḷḷiyil (tām payiṉṟa paḷḷi) utavi āciriyarāyp paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1955 (1924)	:	matippu mikkatākak karutappaṭṭa maturait tamiḻc caṅkamnaṭattiya paṇṭitat tērvil veṟṟi peṟṟār.(ï› vāṇṭil pāvāṇarait tavira vēṟoruvarumveṟṟi peṟavillai).
		utavittamiḻāciriyar, ceṉṉai, tiruvallik kēṇik kellaṟṟu uyarnilaip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1956 (1925)	:	tamiḻāciriyar, ceṉṉaik kiṟittavak kallūri uyarnilaip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
		ciṟuvar pāṭal tiraṭṭu nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1957 (1926)	:	tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiyat tamiḻc caṅkam naṭattiya taṉittamiḻp pulavar tērvil ivar oruvarē veṟṟi peṟṟār eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
ti.pi. 1959 (1928)	:	talaimait tamiḻāciriyar, maṉṉārkuṭi piṉlē kallūri uyarnilaip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
		mutal maṉaivi ecuttar ammaiyār maṟaivu.
ti.pi. 1961 (1930)	:	nēcamaṇi ammaiyārai maṇantār.
ti.pi. 1963 (1932)			kiṟittavak kīrttaṉam - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1965 (1934)	:	talaimait tamiḻāciriyar, picappu īpar uyarnilaip paḷḷi, puttūr,  tiruccirāppaḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟi ṉār.
		kīḻaikkalait (B.O.L.) tērvil veṟṟi peṟṟār.
ti.pi. 1968 (1937)	:	mutal inti etirppup pōrai maiyamākak koṇṭu - centamiḻkkāñci nūl veḷi yīṭu. inti etirppuk kiḻamai koṇṭāṭiṉār.
		``kaṭṭuraik kacaṭaṟai eṉṉum viyāca viḷakkam''nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1971 (1940)	:	oppiyaṉ moḻinūl mutaṟpākam
		iyaṟṟamiḻilakkaṇamākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
		kaṭṭurai varaiviyal eṉṉum urainaṭai          ilakkaṇa nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1972 (1941)	:	``tamiḻar carittirac curukkam''  veḷiyīṭu : tamiḻaka iḷaiñar maṉṟam, tiruccirāppaḷḷi.
		``tamiḻaṉ eppaṭik keṭṭāṉ'' ākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭal.
ti.pi. 1974 (1943)	:	talaimait tamiḻāciriyar, ceṉṉai muttiyālup pēṭṭai uyarnilaip paḷḷiyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
	:	cuṭṭu viḷakkam - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		- paṇṭitamaṇi katirēcaṉār talaimaiyil naṭai peṟṟa mutalām tamiḻ uṇarcci mānāṭṭil paṅkēṟṟu uraiyāṟṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1975 (1944)	:	- tamiḻttuṟait talaivarākac cēlam nakarāṇmaik kallūriyil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
		- tiraviṭattāy - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1980 (1949)	:	collārāyccik kaṭṭuraikaḷ - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		pāvāṇarkkut tavattiru maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār cāṉṟitaḻ vaḻaṅkic ciṟappittār.
		pāvāṇar avarkaḷ cēlam kallūriyil paṇi yāṟṟiyapōtu teṉmoḻi āciriyar peruñcittira ṉār avarkaḷ akkallūriyil payiṉṟār eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
ti.pi. 1981 (1950)	:	uyartarak kaṭṭurai ilakkaṇam (mu.pā.) - nūl veḷiyīṭu.

ti.pi. 1983 (1952)	:	ceṉṉaippalkalaikkaḻakattil kalaimutuvar (M.A.) paṭṭam peṟṟār.
		paḻantamiḻāṭci - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1984 (1953)	:	mutaṟṟāymoḻi allatu tamiḻākka viḷakkam - nūl veḷiyīṭu.

		aṇṇāmalai palkalaikkaḻakattil virivurai yāḷarākap paṇiyiṟ cērntār.
ti.pi. 1988 (1957)	:	periyār ī.vē.rā. talaimaiyil cēlam tamiḻp pēravai ivariṉ toṇṭaip pārāṭṭit `tirāviṭa moḻinūl ñāyiṟu' eṉum paṭṭam vaḻaṅkic ciṟappittatu.
		ticampar 27, 28, 29-l tilliyil naṭaipeṟṟa aṉait tintiyak kīḻaikkalai mānāṭṭil paṅkēṟṟuc ciṟappittār.
ti.pi.1990 (1959)	:	tamiḻnāṭṭu araciṉ āṭcit tuṟaiyil kalaic col lākkattokuppil paṅkēṟṟuc ciṟappittamaik kākat tamiḻnāṭṭaracu cārpil tamiḻnāṭṭu āḷunarāl  avarukkuc ceppup paṭṭayam vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
		moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar avarkaḷāl peyar cūṭṭappaṭṭa `teṉmoḻi' itaḻ pāvalarēṟu peruñ cittiraṉār avarkaḷāl toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. taṉittamiḻiyakka vaḷarccikku iv vitaḻ iṉṟaḷavum perum paṅkāṟṟi varukiṟatu.
ti.pi. 1992 (1961)	:	ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyiṉ cīrkēṭu - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		- aṇṇāmalai palkalaikkaḻakattil ēṟpaṭṭa cikkalāl eṉakku vaṟumaiyum uṇṭu, maṉaiviyum, makkaḷum uṇṭu - atōṭu eṉakku māṉamum uṇṭu - eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭup palkalaikkaḻakap paṇiyiliruntu veḷiyēṟiṉār. eṉṉōṭu tamiḻum veḷiyēṟiyatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
ti.pi. 1994 (1963)	:	tuṇaiviyār nēcamaṇi ammaiyār maṟaivu.
ti.pi. 1995 (1964)	:	muṉaivar ci.ilakkuvaṉār talaimaiyilāṉa maturait tamiḻk kāppuk kaḻakam - tamiḻp peruṅ kāvalar eṉṉum paṭṭam vaḻaṅkic ciṟappittatu.
		``eṉ aṇṇāmalainakar vāḻkkai'' eṉṉum kaṭṭurait toṭar teṉmoḻiyil veḷivantatu.
ti.pi. 1997 (1966)	:	icaittamiḻk kalampakam

		``The Primary Classical Language of the World'' eṉṉum nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.

		vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu
		“The Language Problem of Tamilnadu and Its Logical Solution” ākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 1999 (1968)	:	maturait tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar maṉṟam ivariṉ maṇiviḻāvaik koṇṭāṭi moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar eṉum paṭṭam vaḻaṅkic ciṟappittatu.
		06-10-1968-l ivarait talaivarākak koṇṭu ulakat tamiḻk kaḻakam tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭatu.
		intiyāl tamiḻ evvāṟu keṭum?
		vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūliṉ vaḻuviyal - 
		``Is Hindi the logical solution of India''
		ākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 2000 (1969)	:	paṟampukkuṭiyil ulakat tamiḻk kaḻaka mutal mānāṭu. im mānāṭṭil tavattiru kuṉṟakkuṭi aṭikaḷār muṉaivar ci. ilakkuvaṉār, muṉaivar va. cupa. māṇikkaṉār, pulavar kuḻantai uḷḷiṭṭa tamiḻc cāṉṟōr paṅkēṟṟuc ciṟappittamai kuṟippiṭat takkatu.
		im mānāṭṭil - tirukkuṟaḷ tamiḻ marapurai
		icaiyaraṅku iṉṉicaik kōvai
		“jÄœ fl‹bfh©L jiH¡Fkh?'' - ākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 2002 (1971)	:	paṟampumalaiyiltavattiru kuṉṟakkuṭi aṭikaḷār talaimaiyil naṭaipeṟṟapāri viḻāvilpāvāṇar centamiḻñāyiṟu eṉṟu pārāṭṭic ciṟappikkap peṟṟār.
		pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷāl teṉ moḻiyil aṟivikkappaṭṭa `centamiḻc coṟ piṟappiyal akaramutalit tiṭṭam' vakukkap peṟṟatu.
ti.pi. 2003 (1972)	:	tañcaiyil ivar talaimaiyil ulakat tamiḻkkaḻaka mānāṭu - tamiḻaṉ piṟantakat tīrmāṉippu mānāṭāka naṭantatu.
		- tamiḻar varalāṟu, tamiḻar matam ākiya nūlkaḷ veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 2004 (1973)	:	 vērccoṟ kaṭṭuraikaḷ nūl veḷiyīṭu.
ti.pi. 2005 (1974)	:	8.5.1974-l centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akara mutalit tiṭṭa iyakkunarākat tamiḻnāṭṭaracāl aṉṟaiya mutalvar kalaiñar mu.karuṇāniti avarkaḷāl amarttappaṭṭār.
		centamiḻc coṟ piṟappiyal pērakara mutaliyiṉ mutal maṭala mutaṟ pakuti avariṉ maṟaiviṟkup piṟaku 1985-l veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu.
ti.pi. 2009 (1978)	:	maṇṇil viṇ allatu vaḷḷuvar kūṭṭuṭaimai - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		ceṉṉaiyil ulakat tamiḻk kaḻaka nāṉkāvatu mānāṭu pāvāṇar muṉṉilaiyil naṭaipeṟṟatu. mānāṭṭiṟkup patippāciriyar pulavar a.nakkīraṉār talaimai tāṅkiṉār.
ti.pi. 2010 (1979)	:	tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu - nūl veḷiyīṭu.
		vāḻnāḷ muḻuvataiyum tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum, tamiḻ ārāyccikkum otukkiya ip perumakaṉukkut tamiḻaka mutalvar puraṭcit talaivar ṭākṭar em.ji.ār avarkaḷāl `centamiḻc celvar' eṉṉum cīriya virutu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu.
ti.pi. 2011 (1980)	:	`Lemurian Language and Its Ramifications - An Epitome' eṉum āṅkila nūl maturaiyil naṭai peṟavirunta aintām ulakattamiḻ mānāṭṭu ayal nāṭṭup pērāḷarkaḷukkāka (52 pakka aḷavil) uruvākkappaṭṭatu.
ti.pi. 2012 (1981)	:	maturaiyil naṭaipeṟṟa aintām ulakattamiḻ mānāṭṭil paṅkēṟṟu māntaṉ tōṟṟamum, tamiḻar marapum eṉṉum talaippil tamiḻartam varalāṟṟup perumaiyai nilaināṭṭip pēciṉār.
		cuṟavam 2ām nāḷ caṉavari 15-l iravu 12.30-kku ivvulaka vāḻviliruntu maṟaintār.
tāymoḻippaṟṟē talaiyāyappaṟṟu
tāymoḻip paṟṟillāt taṉṉāṭṭup paṟṟē
	taṉṉiṉaṅ kollavē tāṉkoṇṭa puṟṟē

moḻiyoṉ ṟillāmalē iṉamoṉṟu millai
	iṉamoṉṟil lāmalē nāṭoṉṟu millai!

matiyuṇar viṉṟiyē maṭiveṉun tūkkam
	mayaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟāy maṟutteḻu tamiḻā!

karantum aṇṭaivīṭṭuḷ kālvaitta liṉṟik
	kaṇmūṭit tūṅkuvāy kaṭiteḻu tamiḻā!

varumāṉaṅ kuṉṟiyē vaṟiyava ṉāṉāy
	vāḻnāḷ vīṇākāmal valleḻu tamiḻā!

arucuvai yuṇṭiyē ākkiṭiṉ uṉkai
	arunta maṟukkiṉṟār āynteḻu tamiḻā!

oliyoṭu variyumpiṉ oḻiyavē aṇmai
	uṟumtēva nākari uṇarnteḻu tamiḻā!

pārmutal paṇpāṭu payiṉṟavaṉ tamiḻaṉ
	palaraiyum uṟaveṉap pakarntavaṉ tamiḻaṉ.

piṟappālē ciṟappillai tamiḻā - itaip
	perunāva laṉcoṉṉāṉ tamiḻā!

maṟattālum tiṟattālum tamiḻā - mēṉmai
	matiyaṟi voḻukkattāl tamiḻā!

tamiḻiṉuk kulakiṉil takuvatē talaimai
	tamiḻarum aṭaiyavē tāḻvilā nilaimai
imiḻtaru moḻiyiyal eytuka nalamē
	ematumey varalāṟē eḻukavē valamē.

vēṉiliṟ kāṉmalai vemmaikoḷ pālaiyiṉ
	viḷainila māvatin nāṭu

miṉṉumpal maṇikaḷum mikuvilai yāṟpeṟa
	mēlulakum virumpum nāṭu

koṉṟutaṉ makaṉaiyē koṭumaiyai nimirttucceṅ
	kōṉmuṟai kulaviya nāṭu

nīliyiṉ kaṇavaṟku nikaḻttiya vāymoḻi
	niṟaivēṟṟiṉa vēḷāḷar nāṭu

aṟiyātu muracaṇai ayarntiṭum pulavarkkum
	ālavaṭṭam viciṟum nāṭu

cittariṉ maruttuvam ciṟantapoṉ ṉākkamum
	cilampoṭu tikaḻntatin nāṭu
pāvāṇar poṉmoḻikaḷ
māntaṉeṉak kumarimalai maruviyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
māṇpuṭaiya nākarikam malarntavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
moḻivaḷarcci mutaṉmutalāy muṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
mōṉaiyuṭaṉ ciṟantaceyyuḷ pēciyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
tuṟainakarāl kaṭalvaṇikam tōṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
piṟanilattu vaṇikaraiyum pēṇiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
toṉmaiyoṭu muṉmai; toṉmaiyoṭu naṉmai;
tāymaiyoṭu tūymai; taḻuviḷamai vaḷamai.
pakuttaṟivē māṉamuṭaṉ paṭaittavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
pakuttaṟivāl tiṇaivakutta paṇpuṭaiyāṉ tamiḻaṉē!
paṉimalaiyai mutaṉmutaṟkaip paṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
palamuṟaimīṉ pulivilatiṟ patittavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
palakalaiyum palanūlum payiṟṟiyavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
palapoṟiyum matilaraṇiṟ patittavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
irutiṇaikkum īntuvakkum iṉpamuṟṟāṉ tamiḻaṉē!
ītalicai yāviṭattē iṟantavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
kaṭalnaṭuvē kalañceluttik karaikaṇṭavaṉ tamiḻaṉē!
kalappaṭaiyāl kuṇattīvaik kāttavaṉum tamiḻaṉē!
pakuttaṟivaic caṟṟum payaṉpaṭuttār kalvi
mikuttataṉāl uṇṭō payaṉ?
tamiḻayarat tāḻntāṉ tamiḻaṉ avaṉē
tamiḻuyarat tāṉuyarvāṉ tāṉ.
tamiḻā uṉṟaṉ muṉṉavaṉē
talaiyāy vāḻnta teṉṉavaṉē
amiḻtām māri aṉṉavaṉē
aḻakāy mutaṉūl coṉṉavaṉē.
paḵṟuḷi nāṭṭiṟ piṟantavaṉām
pakuttaṟivup paṇpiṟ ciṟantavaṉām
pakaiyām malaiyai uṟaḻntavaṉām
pālum puliyiṟ kaṟantavaṉām.
aṉpeṉpatu ēcuvum puttarumpōl ellāriṭattum kāṭṭum nēyam.
āṭci oppumai naṭpuṟaviṟkē aṉṟi aṭimaittaṉattiṟku ētuvākātu.
ōyvakavaiyait tīrmāṉikkum aḷavaiyāy irukka vēṇṭiyatu paṇit tiṟamaiyēyaṉṟi akavai varampaṉṟu.
tuṟavu tammāliyaṉṟavarai potumakkaṭkut toṇṭu ceyvatu ciṟanta tuṟavākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
intiyāvil vilaṅkiṉam irukkum nilaimaiyai uṇartta avaṟṟiṉ kaḻuttuppuṇṇum vilāvelumput tōṟṟamum pōtum.
kaṭṭuppāṭṭillāviṭiṉ kāvalaṉuṅ kāvāṉātalālum ceṅkōlāṭciyoṭu kūṭiya irukaṭciyaracē kuṭiyaraciṟ kēṟṟatām.
piṟamoḻi pēcum ciṟupāṉmaiyar piḷḷaikaḷum avvan nāṭṭup perumpāṉmai moḻiyaiyē kaṟṟal vēṇṭum.... 
kāl moḻivāri mānilap pirivu eṉpatu poruḷaṟṟatum payaṉaṟṟatumām.
māntaṉ vāḻkkai, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam eṉa iruvakaittu. maṉaiviyōṭu kūṭi illattiliruntu ataṟkuriya aṟañ ceytu vāḻum vāḻkkai illaṟam; ulakap paṟṟait tuṟantu ataṟkuriya aṟattōṭu kūṭik kāṭṭil tavañ ceytu vāḻum vāḻkkai tuṟavaṟam.
oruvaṉ illattil iruntu maṉaiyāḷōṭu kūṭivāḻiṉum aṟañ ceyyātu iruppiṉ avaṉ vāḻkkai illaṟamākātu veṟumaṉāṉa ilvāḻkkaiyām.
ilaṅkaiyil iṭarppaṭum makkaḷ perumpālum tamiḻarāyiruttaliṉ avarkaḷiṉ urimaiyaip pēṇikkāttaṟku aṅkuḷḷa intiyat tūtāṇmaik kuḻuttalaivar tamiḻarākavē iruttal vēṇṭum.
karuttuvēṟupāṭṭiṟ kiṭantantu oru cārārai orucārār paḻikkātum pakaikkātum iruppatē uṇmaiyāṉa pakuttaṟivām.
iv vulakil tamiḻaṉaip pōl muṉpu uyarntavaṉu millai; piṉpu tāḻntavaṉum illai.
iṟṟait tamiḻaruṭ perumpālārum tammait tāmē tāḻttuvatilum, iṉattāraip pakaittup pakaivarai vāḻavaippatilum, pakaivar    maṉaṅkuḷirat tam muṉṉōraip paḻippatilum, tammarumait tāymoḻiyaip puṟakkaṇittup pakaivariṉ araicceyaṟkaik kalavai moḻiyaip pōṟṟuvatilum oppuyarvaṟṟavarāy uḻalkiṉṟaṉar.
tantaiyum aracum
oru kuṭumpattiṟ piṟanta piḷḷaikaṭkellām ūṇuṭai yuṟaiyuḷ aḷikkat tantai kaṭamaippaṭṭiruppatu pōṉṟē, oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta kuṭikaṭkellām vēlaiyum pātukāppum aḷikka aracu kaṭamaippaṭṭuḷḷatu.
kōvil vaḻipāṭum iruvakaic caṭaṅkukaḷum tamiḻil naṭaipeṟṟāloḻiya, tamiḻ viṭutalaiyaṭaintu taṉ paḻamperumaiyai mīḷap peṟa muṭiyātu. tamiḻuyarntāltāṉ tamiḻaṉ uyarvāṉ.
antaṇaṉ aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ mutaṉmutal tamiḻakat tuṟaviyaraiyē kuṟittatu pōṉṟu pārppāṉ eṉṉum peyarum mutaṉmutal tamiḻap pūcāriyaiyē kuṟittatu.
tamiḻaṉ eṉṉum iṉam tamiḻpaṟṟiyatē yātalāl. tamiḻ tōṉṟiya iṭamē tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamām. atu teṉvāriyil mūḻkip pōṉa kumarināṭē.
2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta mukamatiyar ciṟupāṉmaiyarēṉum āṅkilar nīṅkiyavuṭaṉ tamakkeṉak kōṉmai (Soveriegnity) koṇṭa taṉināṭu peṟṟuviṭṭaṉarē. nūṟāyiram āṇṭiṟku muṉṉamē tōṉṟi orukāl nāvalantēyam muḻutum āṇṭa paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa tamiḻar ēṉ tam nāṭṭaiyum peṟavillai?..... itaṟkuk karaṇiyam, mukamatiyarkkuḷḷa ōriṉa vuṇarcciyum oṟṟumaiyum maṟamum māṉamum iṟṟait tamiḻaruṭ perumpālārkku eḷḷaḷavum iṉmaiyēyām.
ellāk kēṭukaḷum pāṭukaḷum tuṉpaṅkaḷum tollaikaḷum nīṅki ellārum iṉpamāka vāḻavēṇṭu meṉṉum iṉṉaruḷ nōkkam koṇṭē teḷḷiya maṉamum oḷḷiya aṟivum tiṇṇiya neñcum nuṇṇiya matiyum koṇṭa tiruvaḷḷuvar tirukkuṟaḷai iyaṟṟiṉār.
paṇanāṭṭamiṉṟi, ellārum tattamakkiyaṉṟa paṇiyaic ceytu, ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum otta urimaiyōṭu payaṉpaṭutti oṟṟumaiyāka oruṅkē vāḻum kuṭumpa allatu kūṭṭuk kuṭumpa vāḻkkaiyē potuvuṭaimai yākum.
oru kuṭumpattiṟ piṟanta ellārkkum eṅṅaṉam tiṟamaikkut takka paṇiyum tēvaikkut takka nukarcciyum uṇṭō, aṅṅaṉamē oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟanta ellārkkum iruttal vēṇṭum. ituvē pāttuṇṭal eṉṉum vaḷḷuvar kūṭṭuṭaimai.

